Replace placeholders with values from patient_intake.json\n2. Calculate Korean age and add after birth date\n3. Normalize phone number format\n4. Remove layout cache elements from modified paragraphs\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport zipfile\nimport shutil\nimport os\nimport xml.etree.ElementTree as ET\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\n# Register namespaces to avoid ns0: prefix pollution\nnamespaces = {\n    'hp': 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'\n}\nfor prefix, uri in namespaces.items():\n    ET.register_namespace(prefix, uri)\n\ndef load_patient_data(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load patient data from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef calculate_korean_age(birth_date_str, visit_date_str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Korean full-year age (만 나이 / international age).\n    Age = visit_year - birth_year - 1 if birthday hasn't occurred yet, else visit_year - birth_year\n    \"\"\"\n    birth_date = datetime.strptime(birth_date_str, '%Y-%m-%d')\n    visit_date = datetime.strptime(visit_date_str, '%Y-%m-%d')\n\n    age = visit_date.year - birth_date.year\n\n    # If birthday hasn't occurred yet in the visit year, subtract 1\n    if (visit_date.month, visit_date.day) < (birth_date.month, birth_date.day):\n        age -= 1\n\n    return age\n\ndef normalize_phone(phone_str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize phone number to 000-0000-0000 format.\n    Strip all non-digits and reformat.\n    \"\"\"\n    digits = ''.join(c for c in phone_str if c.isdigit())\n\n    if len(digits) == 11:\n        # Korean mobile: 010-0000-0000\n        return f\"{digits[:3]}-{digits[3:7]}-{digits[7:]}\"\n    elif len(digits) == 10:\n        # Could be 02-0000-0000 (Seoul) or 0XX-000-0000 (regional)\n        if digits.startswith('02'):\n            return f\"{digits[:2]}-{digits[2:6]}-{digits[6:]}\"\n        else:\n            return f\"{digits[:3]}-{digits[3:6]}-{digits[6:]}\"\n    else:\n        # Return as-is if unexpected format\n        return phone_str\n\ndef extract_hwpx(hwpx_path, extract_dir):\n    \"\"\"Extract HWPX file to directory.\"\"\"\n    os.makedirs(extract_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(hwpx_path, 'r') as zf:\n        zf.extractall(extract_dir)\n\ndef create_hwpx(source_dir, hwpx_path):\n    \"\"\"Create HWPX file from directory contents.\"\"\"\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(hwpx_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(source_dir):\n            for file in files:\n                filepath = os.path.join(root, file)\n                arcname = os.path.relpath(filepath, source_dir)\n                zf.write(filepath, arcname)\n\ndef process_xml_section(xml_path, patient_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process section XML file:\n    1. Replace all placeholders\n    2. Add age after birth date\n    3. Normalize phone number\n    4. Normalize phone\n    normalized_phone = normalize_phone(patient_data['회신전화'])\n\n    # Create mapping: placeholder -> replacement value\n    placeholder_map = {\n        '{{환자명}}': patient_data['환자명'],\n        '{{생년월일}}': patient_data['생년월일'] + ' ' + age_str,\n        '{{방문일}}': patient_data['방문일'],\n        '{{진료팀}}': patient_data['진료팀'],\n        '{{회신전화}}': normalized_phone,\n        '{{증상요약}}': patient_data['증상요약'],\n        '{{보험상태}}': patient_data['보험상태'],\n    }\n\n    # Track which paragraphs were modified\n    modified_paras = set()\n\n    # Find all text elements and replace placeholders\n    hp_ns = '{http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML}'\n\n    # First pass: collect text content from all runs in each paragraph\n    for para in root.findall(f'.//{hp_ns}p'):\n        para_id = para.get('id')\n\n        # Collect all text runs in this paragraph\n        text_runs = []\n        for run in para.findall(f'.//{hp_ns}run'):\n            for t_elem in run.findall(f'{hp_ns}t'):\n                text_runs.append(t_elem)\n\n        if not text_runs:\n            continue\n\n        # Get full concatenated text\n        full_text = ''.join(t.text or '' for t in text_runs)\n\n        # Check if any placeholder exists in this paragraph\n        modified = False\n        new_text = full_text\n        for placeholder, replacement in placeholder_map.items():\n            if placeholder in new_text:\n                new_text = new_text.replace(placeholder, replacement)\n                modified = True\n\n        if modified:\n            modified_paras.add(para_id)\n            # Put all text in first run, clear others\n            if text_runs:\n                text_runs[0].text = new_text\n                for t_elem in text_runs[1:]:\n                    t_elem.text = ''\n\n    # Second pass: remove layout cache elements from modified paragraphs\n    cache_elements = [f'{hp_ns}linesegarray', f'{hp_ns}lineSegArray', f'{hp_ns}lineSeg']\n\n    for para in root.findall(f'.//{hp_ns}p'):\n        para_id = para.get('id')\n        if para_id in modified_paras:\n            # Remove all layout cache elements\n            for cache_tag in cache_elements:\n                for cache_elem in para.findall(cache_tag):\n                    para.remove(cache_elem)\n\n    # Write modified XML\n    tree.write(xml_path, encoding='UTF-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n    return True\n\ndef verify_no_placeholders(xml_path):\n    \"\"\"Verify no {{ placeholders remain in XML.\"\"\"\n    with open(xml_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n    return '{{' not in content\n\ndef main():\n    # Paths\n    template_path = '/root/clinic_intake_template.hwpx'\n    patient_json_path = '/root/patient_intake.json'\n    output_path = '/root/clinic_intake_ready.hwpx'\n    work_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_work_processed'\n\n    # Clean up work directory if exists\n    if os.path.exists(work_dir):\n        shutil.rmtree(work_dir)\n\n    # Load patient data\n    patient_data = load_patient_data(patient_json_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded patient data: {patient_data}\")\n\n    # Calculate and display age\n    age = calculate_korean_age(patient_data['생년월일'], patient_data['방문일'])\n    print(f\"Calculated age: {age}세\")\n\n    # Normalize phone\n    normalized_phone = normalize_phone(patient_data['회신전화'])\n    print(f\"Normalized phone: {normalized_phone}\")\n\n    # Extract template\n    extract_hwpx(template_path, work_dir)\n    print(f\"Extracted template to {work_dir}\")\n\n    # Process section XML\n    section_xml = os.path.join(work_dir, 'Contents', 'section0.xml')\n    process_xml_section(section_xml, patient_data)\n    print(f\"Processed section XML\")\n\n    # Verify no placeholders remain\n    if verify_no_placeholders(section_xml):\n        print(\"✓ Verified: No placeholders remain in section XML\")\n    else:\n        print(\"✗ ERROR: Placeholders still remain!\")\n        with open(section_xml, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            content = f.read()\n        print(\"Content:\", content)\n        return\n\n    # Create output HWPX\n    create_hwpx(work_dir, output_path)\n    print(f\"Created output HWPX: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Final verification\n    print(\"\\n--- Final Verification ---\")\n\n    # Extract output and check\n    verify_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_verify'\n    if os.path.exists(verify_dir):\n        shutil.rmtree(verify_dir)\n    extract_hwpx(output_path, verify_dir)\n\n    verify_xml = os.path.join(verify_dir, 'Contents', 'section0.xml')\n    with open(verify_xml, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n\n    checks = [\n        ('No placeholders', '{{' not in content),\n        ('Patient name appears', patient_data['환자명'] in content),\n        ('Age note appears', f'({age}세)' in content),\n        ('Normalized phone', normalized_phone in content),\n        ('Birth date', patient_data['생년월일'] in content),\n        ('Visit date', patient_data['방문일'] in content),\n        ('Medical team', patient_data['진료팀'] in content),\n        ('Symptom summary', patient_data['증상요약'] in content),\n        ('Insurance status', patient_data['보험상태'] in content),\n        ('Korean label preserved', '환자 접수 요약' in content),\n        ('Signature note preserved', '수기 서명' in content),\n    ]\n\n    all_passed = True\n    for check_name, result in checks:\n        status = \"✓\" if result else \"✗\"\n        print(f\"  {status} {check_name}\")\n        if not result:\n            all_passed = False\n\n    if all_passed:\n        print(\"\\n✓ All checks passed! birth_year - 1 if birthday hasn't occurred yet, else visit_year - birth_year\n    \"\"\"\n    birth_date = datetime.strptime(birth_date_str, '%Y-%m-%d')\n    visit_date = datetime.strptime(visit_date_str, '%Y-%m-%d')\n\n    age = visit_date.year -
